# v5.15.5.F.4d.1.E sub-sprint — architecture decisions v2

Captures architectural decisions from the planning conversation 2026-05-28. Decisions span the full `.E` sub-sprint (`.E.1` through `.E.8` + standalone `.E.X` for hot-reload).

## Decisions (operator-decided)

<!-- D/C/F: D-1 -->
**D-1: Architecture choice — Architecture E++.** Per-node sub-accounts + multi-exchange substrate + headless service + io_uring + WS-API + DPDK (end-game). Phased delivery option D (~6-9 weeks; 8+ sub-ships). Pursuing perfect-architecture path; time-irrelevant; ego-flex motivation accepted.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-2 -->
**D-2: Account topology — real sub-accounts preferred (canonical mode).** Virtual partition becomes fallback for exchanges without sub-account support (Alpaca, IBKR retail). Sub-account isolation is structurally stronger (Binance-enforced); per-node failure domain at economic layer.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-3 -->
**D-3: Multi-exchange — yes, FOREACH_EXCHANGE registry from day one.** Framework substrate lands at `.E.1` even though only Binance wired. Future exchanges (Alpaca, IBKR, future) become 1-row-in-registry additions. Per-node bound to one exchange (Mode 1).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-4 -->
**D-4: Headless service architecture — folded into `.E.2` (not deferred to `.E.7`).** Engine binary stripped of GUI; runs as systemd service 24/7 in production. State published via mmap'd shared memory; commands via Unix domain socket. Multiple concurrent read-only viewers OK. Crash recovery via mmap'd state file. SSH-tunneled UDS for remote attach (no HTTPS; SSH baked-in security wins).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-5 -->
**D-5: Per-node config files — separate folders per node (no template inheritance).** Each node gets `configs/clusters/<exchange>/nodes/node_<id>/{core.cfg, strategy.cfg, ml.cfg, observability.cfg, notes.md}`. Self-contained; copy-paste-friendly; independent versioning. Replaces monolithic engine.cfg for per-node surfaces. X-macro FOREACH_PER_NODE_CFG_FIELD registry still drives parser schema; just file I/O layer changes.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-6 -->
**D-6: ML training goes headless — `foxml-train` CLI tool.** Replaces foxml_suite GUI for production training. Config-file-driven (full training pipeline reproducible from one cfg). foxml_suite deprecated. Jupyter notebooks for interactive ML exploration; CLI for production.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-7 -->
**D-7: GUI focus reduced — viewer becomes pure read-only + command sender.** Engine doesn't depend on GUI being running. fox-tui (notcurses; reads mmap directly) becomes primary monitoring interface. fox-cli for control commands. Existing Dear ImGui GUI hard-deprecated at `.E.2` (engine_gui binary archived to legacy/).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-8 -->
**D-8: Hot-reload — both layers (config + strategy code).** Layer 1 (config reload via SIGUSR1; slow→hot seqlock update of parameters) lands `.E.2`. Layer 2 (.so reload via dlopen; per-node slow-path swaps function pointer atomically) lands standalone `.E.X`. Both compatible with H7 (hot path branchless via cached pointer) + H3 (atomic-only; no mutex).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-9 -->
**D-9: Public repo stays public (FoxML_Trader_v2 = AGPL).** Architecture isn't competitive secret; execution + tuning is. Hedge-fund visibility per `user_public_work_attracts_hedge_funds` retained. Gitignore tightened for new operator-specific surfaces (per-node configs in `configs/clusters/`; secrets; runtime state).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-10 -->
**D-10: SSH preferred over HTTPS for operator control channel.** Public-key auth + encrypted transport + no certificate management. Local UDS direct; remote SSH-tunneled UDS. HTTPS dropped from scope. Prometheus `/metrics` HTTP endpoint exists for Grafana scrape but localhost-only (firewall + SSH-tunneled scraper).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-11 -->
**D-11: Monitoring — Grafana (remote/historical) + custom TUI (local/real-time) — both, complementary.** Engine emits metrics in mmap (TUI reads zero-copy) + Prometheus exposition format (Grafana scrapes via HTTP endpoint). Operator runs Prometheus + Grafana separately as standard infrastructure. No vendor lock-in.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-12 -->
**D-12: R1 mitigation — proceed without empirical (option c).** Caramel doesn't care about paper-test-before-decision; architectural win is justification enough. Skip paper-test session insertion between .D and .E.1.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-13 -->
**D-13: `--deep` flag drift in 5 SKILL.md files — RESOLVED.** Removed `--deep` from 3 bash invocations in capture-audit/SKILL.md:157, close-session/SKILL.md:34, accept-handoff/SKILL.md:146. Tool's canonical flag space stays `--since/--strict/--json/--include-archived`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-14 -->
**D-14: Engine version + software version decoupled.** Version.hpp gets two fields: ENGINE_VERSION_STRING (per-ship internal; high-velocity) + SOFTWARE_VERSION_STRING (platform milestone; coarse-grained). Software version starts at v0.1.0 for full E end-game. Both shown in fox-tui status bar; both stamped into HMAC body.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-15 -->
**D-15: Cluster framing adopted.** Per-exchange = cluster. Cluster owns producer + adapter + WS user-data + sub-account pool + connection topology + rate budget. Nodes are members of a cluster bound 1:1 to a sub-account. Deployment > Cluster > Node hierarchy.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-16 -->
**D-16: TUI library — notcurses.** Best modern terminal support (sixel inline charts; truecolor; mouse; tab support; clean API; actively maintained). Vi-style keybinding (h/j/k/l + colon commands); operator-customizable.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-17 -->
**D-17: Binary naming — fox-engine + fox-tui + fox-cli + foxml-train.** Linux_Theme collision check confirms clean (Linux_Theme uses fox-* but not these specific names). foxml_suite deprecated.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-18 -->
**D-18: State persistence cfg-driven {max | more | standard | relaxed | minimal | none}.** 6-mode enum. Default `standard` (per-fill + 100ms tick aggregates). Production `max`. Backtest `none`. Per-cluster override possible.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-19 -->
**D-19: Prometheus = localhost-only bind (127.0.0.1:9091) + SSH-tunneled scrape.** No external firewall hole. Standard ops; secure default.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-20 -->
**D-20: systemd unit as deployment template (engine deployment-agnostic).** Engine binary doesn't care if systemd-managed or direct-launched. Template `installation/fox-engine.service` provided; operator copies to /etc/systemd/system/ for server deployment. Laptop dev = direct launch.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-21 -->
**D-21: Audit log = JSONL with optional SHA-256 hash chain.** Categories split: `trades.jsonl` / `state-changes.jsonl` / `commands.jsonl` / `errors.jsonl` / `metrics-checkpoint.jsonl`. Rotation by day. Hash-chain opt-in (default off; for tamper-evidence when concerned).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-22 -->
**D-22: Per-node paper mode flag folded into `.E.1`.** `cores/core_<id>/core.cfg → mode = backtest|paper|live|shadow` (4-state enum). Power-user feature: paper-test new strategy live alongside live cores. Hot-swap if performs well. Architecturally trivial; ~1-2 days additional in `.E.1`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-23 -->
**D-23: Meta-controller = C++ (not Python); NOT in `.E` scope; documented for future.** Separate C++ binary (`fox-supervisor`) when it lands. Reads engine state via mmap; sends commands via UDS. Language consistency. Deferred to v5.16+.
<!-- STATUS: deferred (documented for future) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-24 -->
**D-24: Capital reserve = existing pattern extension.** Per-cluster `reserve_pct` + `max_per_node_pct` cfg fields. Aggregator enforces at submit time. No NEW infrastructure; just extends existing OMS risk logic to cluster-level.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-25 -->
**D-25: Per-exchange `is_open?` check at slow-path entry for cross-day position management.** Per-cluster `market_hours` cfg + per-node slow-path check at cycle entry. Closed → per-node sleeps (slow-path cycle skipped; hot-path remains alive for safe drain). Lands `.E.6` Alpaca naturally; infrastructure stub at `.E.1`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-26 -->
**D-26: GUI hard-deprecate at `.E.2`.** engine_gui binary removed from build; Dear ImGui code archived to legacy/. Replaced by fox-tui + fox-cli + Grafana. Clean break.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-27 -->
**D-27: Core → Node hard rename (Option α).** All `state.cores[i]` → `state.nodes[i]`. `MAX_CORES` → `MAX_NODES`. `FOREACH_PER_CORE_CFG_FIELD` → `FOREACH_PER_NODE_CFG_FIELD`. `CoreContext` → `NodeContext`. Per-core dirs → per-node dirs. ~1-2 days mechanical refactor + tests. Lands at `.E.1`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-28 -->
**D-28: Cluster/Node/Deployment hierarchy adopted.** Filesystem: configs/clusters/<exchange>/{cluster.cfg, credentials/, nodes/node_<id>/*.cfg}. Cluster-rooted. NEW DESIGN_SPEC: cluster-node-hierarchy-filesystem-layout-pattern.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-29 -->
**D-29: Detailed plan bodies for all 8 sub-ships drafted now.** Per `feedback_plan_right_not_fast`. No length cap. Unbounded depth. ~3000-5000 lines per plan body. Cross-ship invariants visible at planning time.
<!-- STATUS: pending (draft starting after decision log + dependency graph) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-30 -->
**D-30: Backtest → paper → live transition discipline.** NEW DESIGN_SPEC: backtest-paper-live-convergence-discipline. Codifies 4-step transition (backtest → paper-mode → small-capital live → full live); each step has different drift sources; sub-account isolation supports gradual capital ramp.
<!-- STATUS: pending (lands at .E.1) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-31 -->
**D-31: Boot-time engine preflight check.** Extends LiveReadiness_Verify v5.15.2 pattern. Verifies: connectivity to each cluster's exchange + credentials valid + API key permissions (NO enableWithdrawals) + rate-limit headroom + sub-account binding valid + symbol valid + strategy loadable + hardware features present (SHA-NI, AVX2, TSC). ANY failure → refuse start + emit alert.
<!-- STATUS: pending (lands at .E.1; full set at .E.2) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-32 -->
**D-32: 4-state mode enum for per-node.** `mode = backtest | paper | live | shadow`. backtest = synthetic data; paper = real data + simulated fills; live = real submission; shadow = real submission with kill-switch override (for future colo testing). Per-cluster aggregation respects mode (paper P&L tagged separately).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-33 -->
**D-33: Dev mode vs production thread topology cfg flag.** `topology.mode = dev | production`. Dev = OS-scheduled (laptop works on 8 cores); production = strict isolcpus + nohz_full + per-thread CPU pinning. Same binaries; topology cfg flag toggles. NEW DESIGN_SPEC: dev-vs-production-thread-topology-pattern.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-34 -->
**D-34: 1 global aggregator (not per-cluster).** Reads all node state via seqlock; computes per-cluster + global totals + sets hierarchical kill flags. Per-cluster totals computed within global aggregator. 1 CPU core deployment-wide.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-35 -->
**D-35: 2-cycle audit convergence baseline.** Cycle 1 typically YELLOW with HIGH findings → cycle 2 verifies GREEN after amendments. Cycle 3+ signals META gap per `feedback_iteration_spiral_signals_audit_meta_gap`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-36 -->
**D-36: Test infrastructure layout.** `tests/{unit,integration,chaos,benchmark,property}/`. Unit = controller_test extension. Integration = testnet against Binance + Alpaca paper. Chaos = kill random node thread; verify graceful degradation. Benchmark = latency regression detection per ship. Property = invariant-based tests (e.g., total deployed capital ≤ master).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-37 -->
**D-37: Reconciliation cadence — boot + every 5 min (cfg-overridable).** Detects drift between engine state and exchange truth. Per-node parallel reconcile at boot. Periodic reconcile during operation (configurable cadence).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-38 -->
**D-38: Per-cluster failure isolation.** Cluster halts on prolonged exchange outage (cfg-driven threshold, default 10 min). Other clusters continue trading. Cluster-level kill flag (sister to global + per-node kill flags).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-39 -->
**D-39: Replay determinism via TSC sequence numbers.** Each event tagged with `(cluster_seq, tsc)` at emission. Replay sorts by (cluster_seq, tsc) for cross-cluster ordering. TSC is per-core monotonic + sub-nanosecond; cluster_seq is per-cluster monotonic. Together = total ordering for replay.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-40 -->
**D-40: fox-cli authentication via UDS filesystem permissions.** chmod 600 on socket. Operator group access optional. No tokens; OS handles security.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-41 -->
**D-41: Per-cluster feature registry.** Each cluster has its own FOREACH_FEATURE_<EXCHANGE>. Train-serve parity audit per cluster (M5 discipline extends per-cluster). Per-exchange feature compute respects exchange-specific market microstructure.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-42 -->
**D-42: State file path conventions.** Production: `/var/lib/fox/state/state.bin` (systemd-default). Dev: `~/.local/share/fox/state/state.bin` (user-default). cfg-overridable via `--state-dir`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-43 -->
**D-43: Webhook alerts at `.E.2`.** Per-event-type configurable URL list. Events: drawdown / sub-account-suspended / exchange-down / model-drift / kill-switch-tripped. Engine emits; operator wires webhook → Slack/Discord/email at their infrastructure.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-44 -->
**D-44: Hardware feature check at boot.** Require SHA-NI (HMAC accel) + AVX2 (FPN math) + TSC (timing). Refuse to start if missing. Compiler flags include `-march=native` to confirm at compile + boot.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-45 -->
**D-45: Engine upgrade path = stop-old + start-new (option a).** Safest. Operator: SIGTERM old engine → wait for graceful shutdown → start new engine → reconcile on resume. Documented in OPERATOR_MANUAL.md.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-46 -->
**D-46: Documentation deliverables comprehensive.** At `.E.2` ship close: DESIGN_PHILOSOPHY.md amended (new sections) + CLAUDE.md updated + DEPLOYMENT_GUIDE.md (NEW) + OPERATOR_MANUAL.md (NEW) + ARCHITECTURE_OVERVIEW.md (NEW) + GLOSSARY.md (NEW) + INCIDENT_RUNBOOK.md (NEW) + STRATEGY_LIFECYCLE.md (NEW) + DR_TESTING.md (NEW) + CONTRIBUTING/ (NEW: how to add exchange/strategy/feature).
<!-- STATUS: pending (lands at .E.2) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-47 -->
**D-47: Engine crash restart action cfg-driven.** `on_crash_restart_action = resume | cancel-all | flatten` cfg field. Default `resume` (read mmap state; reconcile against exchange; continue). Operator can choose `cancel-all` (cancel all open orders on restart; positions preserved) or `flatten` (force-flatten all positions on restart; clean slate). Refinement of D-6 default. Granular operator control over post-crash semantics. Lands at `.E.1`. NEW DESIGN_SPEC: `framework-patterns/crash-recovery-action-policy-pattern.md`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-48 -->
**D-48: Production compiler flags include security hardening.** Production build flags: `-O3 -march=native -mtune=native -flto -fwhole-program -funroll-loops -fstack-clash-protection -D_FORTIFY_SOURCE=2 -fstack-protector-strong`. Dev/debug build flags omit security hardening for debugability (`-O0 -g`). AGPL public + hedge-fund-visibility = security matters; security hardening at production build is canonical. Refinement of D-21. Adopting at `.E.1` (build.sh update).
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-49 -->
**D-49: Audit log retention is cfg-driven.** `audit_retention_days` cfg field. Default 90; configurable (30 / 60 / 90 / 180 / 365 / unlimited). `unlimited` mode rotates by size threshold (cfg field `audit_max_file_size_mb`; default 1024 MB). Refinement of D-21 (audit log structure decision); operationalizes retention policy. Lands at `.E.2`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-50 -->
**D-50: Reconciliation cadence is hybrid.** Per-fill triggered reconcile + every 5 min time-based reconcile (cfg-overridable). Per-fill catches drift faster; time-based is safety net. cfg flag `reconcile_per_fill = true | false` (default true) lets operator disable per-fill mode if API weight overhead concern. Per-node parallel reconcile at boot still in. Refinement of D-37. NEW DESIGN_SPEC: `framework-patterns/hybrid-reconciliation-cadence-pattern.md`. Lands at `.E.1`.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-51 -->
**D-51: AVX-512 = WARN-not-block at boot.** Sister to D-44 (hardware feature check). Required: SHA-NI + AVX2 + TSC (refuse to start if missing). Optional: AVX-512 (WARN if not present; logged at boot; engine falls back to scalar path for any AVX-512 hot kernels per FoxLIB H10 discipline). Refinement of D-44; graceful fallback preserves operability on older CPUs.
<!-- STATUS: landed -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-52 -->
**D-52: MANDATORY plan body section — "Required reading + reference docs cross-check".** Every plan body in `.E` sub-sprint includes a dedicated section right after frontmatter, before scope. Sections:
- Anti-patterns this plan avoids (DOCS/RECURRING_BUG_PATTERNS.md Class N references)
- DESIGN_SPECS this plan extends or applies (cross-refs to existing patterns)
- NEW DESIGN_SPECS this plan introduces (Stage 3 first canonical citations)
- TECH_DEBT entries this plan closes (DOCS/TECH_DEBT.md references)
- Memory rules (feedback) this plan applies (memory/*.md citations)
- Required reading before implementation (DOCS/DESIGN_PHILOSOPHY.md sections; STRATEGY_AND_CODING_RULES.md; CLAUDE_INTEGRATION.md; this decision log; dependency graph)
Codifies `feedback_audit_canonical_sister_before_new_infra` as STRUCTURAL plan requirement. Per operator directive "make sure plans involve this, and also point to which reference docs that use and stuff". Prevents repeating known broken approaches; prevents tech-debt accumulation; prevents reintroducing known bugs. Wednesday-night-planning institutional-memory leverage.
<!-- STATUS: landed (template-level discipline; all .E plan bodies inherit) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-53 -->
**D-53: Future-oriented plan template AMENDED to include mandatory D-52 cross-check section.** `DESIGN_SPECS/plan-templates/future-oriented-plan-template.md` updated at `.E.1` to include "Required reading + reference docs cross-check" as default section in template. Benefits all future plans beyond `.E` (template-level change has multiplier effect on doc-discipline). Sister to existing template body. Lands at `.E.1` plan body draft time.
<!-- STATUS: pending (lands with .E.1 plan body) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-54 -->
**D-54: O(1) Event-Sourced Aggregator folded into `.E.1`.** Push-model instead of pull-model: each per-node fill triggers atomic_fetch_add on global aggregator's running aggregate (vs aggregator polling all nodes every 100ms). Aggregator's periodic cycle becomes "verify integrity" not "compute totals". Drops kill-switch latency floor from ~100ms to ~50μs (fill arrival time). Improves performance (O(1) vs O(N×cycles)) + correctness (no race window; aggregator state always exact) + accuracy (per-fill bit-exact totals; no cycle-based aggregation drift). Multi-writer atomic on aligned memory; lock-free; tight contention bounded by fill rate (~20/sec at full tilt = negligible). NEW DESIGN_SPEC: `framework-patterns/event-sourced-aggregator-o1-pattern.md` (Stage 3 first canonical at `.E.1`). Refines D-34 (aggregator placement); supersedes Decision D in plan body (cadence-based → event-driven primary; 100ms cycle becomes integrity-verification only).
<!-- STATUS: landed (fold into .E.1) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-55 -->
**D-55: Portfolio/Position SoA + AVX-512 vectorization partial fold into `.E.1`.** AoS → SoA layout transition for Portfolio + Position structs bundled with NodeState refactor at `.E.1` (incremental cost; cache-locality wins; AVX-512 SIMD natural fit even at small N=8-32 positions). ML feature compute SoA + AVX-512 deferred to `.E.6/.E.7` where it pays off most (per-cluster many features). H10 SIMD discipline already in place; this extends the surface. NEW DESIGN_SPEC: `framework-patterns/portfolio-soa-vectorization-pattern.md` (Stage 2 DRAFT at `.E.1`; Stage 3 first canonical at `.E.6/.E.7` ML feature compute application).
<!-- STATUS: landed (fold into .E.1 for Portfolio/Position; deferred to .E.6/.E.7 for ML feature compute) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-56 -->
**D-56: Asymmetric Blackboard pattern selection criteria documented; no code fold-in.** Already adopted where applicable (seqlock for slow→hot cfg; atomic for kill flags; per-node→aggregator state publication). Forcing onto SPSC event streams would DEGRADE correctness (must-not-miss event log semantics lost). Pattern-selection criteria codified at CLAUDE.md `.E.2` documentation push: "SPSC for must-not-miss event streams; seqlock/atomic blackboard for latest-value-wins state visibility". NEW DESIGN_SPEC: `framework-patterns/spsc-vs-blackboard-selection-criteria.md` (Stage 2 DRAFT at `.E.2`).
<!-- STATUS: landed (documentation only; no code change) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-57 -->
**D-57: DPDK / Onload `.E.8` DEFERRED indefinitely** (per operator clarification 2026-05-28). Operator doesn't have specialized hardware (DPDK-compatible NIC; Solarflare for Onload; kernel-bypass capable network card). Without hardware, `.E.8` is wasted engineering effort. The `.E.4` io_uring + kTLS work is sufficient (works on any Linux 5.6+ kernel without special hardware) for the latency improvement the engine can ACTUALLY achieve on operator's deployment. `.E.8` plan body retained at `subplans/2026-05-28-v5.15.5.F.4d.1.E.8-dpdk-kernel-bypass.md` as FUTURE REFERENCE — re-activate when/if operator acquires DPDK-compatible hardware OR co-locates on cloud instance with DPDK-supported NIC. NEW DESIGN_SPEC `concurrency-patterns/dpdk-userspace-networking-pattern.md` REMAINS Stage 2 DRAFT (no Stage 3 promotion until 1st canonical actually lands).
<!-- STATUS: deferred (out of `.E` sub-sprint scope) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-58 -->
**D-58: Exchange adapter ships (.E.6 Alpaca + .E.7 IBKR) repurposed to GENERIC FRAMEWORK FOCUS** (per operator clarification 2026-05-28). Operator wants "generalizable reusable way to add in different ones as needed" — NOT specific exchange implementations bundled into `.E` sub-sprint. New scope:
- `.E.6` REPURPOSED: "Exchange adapter framework generalization + how-to-add documentation". Lands `DOCS/CONTRIBUTING/add-exchange.md` with step-by-step guide. Validates framework Stage 4 promotion criterion (without requiring specific 2nd canonical to land at THIS ship).
- `.E.7` MOVED to OPTIONAL-FUTURE: IBKR adapter is a 3rd canonical of FOREACH_EXCHANGE; lands when operator decides to add IBKR specifically. Plan body retained at `subplans/2026-05-28-v5.15.5.F.4d.1.E.7-ibkr-exchange.md` as REFERENCE EXAMPLE for adding any FIX-protocol exchange.

<!-- D/C/F: D-59 -->
**D-59: NEW `.E.0` Pre-coding plan audit + verification sub-ship** (per operator suggestion 2026-05-28). Precedes ALL coding work in `.E` sub-sprint. Runs comprehensive audits against each plan body + cross-ship invariants + anti-pattern catalog + DESIGN_SPECS catalog BEFORE any coding starts. Per `feedback_plan_right_not_fast` + `feedback_audit_canonical_sister_before_new_infra` + audit-driven discipline (DESIGN_PHILOSOPHY § 11). Codifies audit-driven discipline at sub-sprint scope (vs per-ship scope). Six audit dimensions per plan: /precoding-audit-gate HIGH-RISK 5-agent + /bug-check + /dod-audit + /parity-check + /anti-spaghetti + cross-ship invariant verification. Plus 4 codebase-wide audits (registry-fit / test-strength / parity / anti-spaghetti). Total: 39 audit firings; ~30-60 min wall time parallel. Per-plan + cross-ship synthesis + operator triage + plan body amendments + cycle 2 verification → GREEN-READY-TO-CODE before `.E.1` coding starts. Effort: ~3-5 days focused (may stretch to 5-7 if findings volume substantial). LOW-RISK (pure audit; no code changes; no architectural commits).
<!-- STATUS: landed (NEW ship; precedes all .E coding) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-60 -->
**D-60: Audit-driven sub-sprint trajectory verification BEFORE coding** (per D-59). Ladder: push every guard to the HIGHEST reachable tier (1 structural/compile-time → 2 CI → 3 test → 4 runtime → 5 cadence-sweep); ONE guard per class, no duplication (max coverage, not max count — guard-sprawl is its own rot); the hot path takes Tier-1 guards ONLY (no runtime checks affordable at 500ns; risk checks live on the drainer/slow path). Triad (the "is it working?" answer): determinism (byte-identical cross-run/cross-binary) + golden-master/characterization tests + built-in observability. <!-- D/C/F: D-72 -->
**D-72: Rolling-window seam cadence for sequential per-plan dives** (operator design 2026-05-28). Plan-by-plan with BOUNDED neighbor-overlap: dive N's window = {inbound seam from N-1} ∪ {N} ∪ {outbound seam to N+1}, where the seam = the forward-promises / cross-ship invariants at that boundary. Keeps the focus / context-isolation benefit while catching "a decision in N silently breaks N+1." It is the DYNAMIC complement to the static dependency DAG (the DAG declares the edges; the window verifies each holds + re-checks if a decision moves a node). = `/seam-audit` (audit-recommended skill, task #20) applied at the PLANNING level; proven necessity — deep-sweep pass-2 (a seam-scan) caught the slow→hot flag-clobber that no per-surface scan could see. The HANDOFF doc carries the seam between dives → the handoff template gets an explicit outbound-seam / inbound-seam structure (extends `/handoff` Stage 2.8 + `/accept-handoff` Stage 4.5; built + proven at the first inter-dive handoff `.E.1`→`.E.2`, same way the `.E`-ship template's first instance was the `.E.0` handoff). Dive order: `.E.1` first (foundation; DAG root; the `conc-5` call). → promote cadence to DESIGN_SPEC (sister to `audit-driven-sub-sprint-trajectory-verification` per D-60) + plan template + `/seam-audit` at Step-3.
<!-- STATUS: drafted (E-guard-coverage-matrix.md §5; handoff-template rework tracked) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-73 -->
**D-73: Phased pre-`.E` foundation sequence locked (the "before any `.E` code" arc).** Synthesizes DD-5 + D-60 + D-68/D-70/D-72 into an explicit, reproducible 5-phase foundation that gates `.E.1` coding. Locked 2026-05-29 (`.E.0` pickup; operator directive "lock shape so it's reproducible + reusable — there's valuable knowledge in how we organized this plan").

**The phases:**
- **Phase 0 — Bedrock** (trust the instrument + the evidence before anything leans on them): validate `/plan-dive` (calibrate on `.E.1` vs `E.1-dive-synthesis.md`) → runtime-confirm the 2 CRITICALs + FP/parity reality on a disposable clone (Phase A2) → land the foundational engine-code fixes the net depends on (`F-056` FPN_Sqrt determinism / `F-058` strict-aliasing UB / `F-059` parity-oracle stub) → scaffold the 3 CI gates (determinism / latency-ratchet / merge-gate per D-71).
- **Phase 1 — Lock current behavior** (the structural no-reintroduction net): PERSIST characterization tests GREEN on the CURRENT engine + codify the 141 findings → NEW Classes 37+ + convert ~97 `ci_tool_candidate` → CI checks + ~122 `test_gap` → regression tests. Built BEFORE the rename so a reintroduced bug/debt is a red build, not a missed audit.
- **Phase 2 — Root-plan GREEN first**: dive + amend `.E.1` ALONE to GREEN before any downstream dive (it is the DAG root; every other plan assumes its substrate, which is currently RED + will shift during amendment).
- **Phase 3 — Rolling-window sweep**: `/plan-dive` `.E.2`→`.E.X` sequentially per D-72 seam cadence; lay each findings-slice fix-design + wire `findings_sidecar:` + fill guard-matrix rows.
- **Phase 4 — GREEN gate**: `.E.0` GREEN (refined below). ONLY THEN `.E.1` coding starts.
- **(during coding) per-ship 2nd net**: each `.E.x` still fires its own `/precoding-audit-gate` + `/bug-check` from the GREEN baseline + `/post-ship-audit` + `/test-strength-audit` at close. Defense-in-depth — the two-layer discipline (sub-sprint gate ON TOP of per-ship gate).

**Three load-bearing refinements:**
1. **The no-reintroduction guarantee is the NET, not the audits.** Audits are Tier-5 sweeps (rely on someone running them); the guarantee is Tier-1/2/3 (compile-time / CI / test) built on the current engine before the rename. "Push every guard to the highest reachable tier" (D-70 ladder). This is the structural answer to "don't reintroduce known debt/bug-classes/findings."
2. **Bedrock before the sweep.** The per-plan dives' findings-ingestion layer trusts confirmed findings + a calibrated instrument → validate-instrument + confirm-evidence (Phase 0) precede the systematic dive sweep, else false GREENs.
3. **Two-phase net (resolves the Discipline-#1 tension).** `.E.0` forbids canonical engine-code changes, but the foundational fixes (`F-056/F-058/F-059`) ARE engine code the net rests on. So: **Net-1** = PERSIST tests for already-stable surfaces (fee/accounting, regime-given-input, parser byte-equiv) → written in `.E.0`; **Net-2** = the foundational fixes + their determinism/characterization tests → a SEPARATE pre-`.E.1` correctness mini-ship (`standalone(pre-.E.1)` findings; NOT rename-owned → safe now; task #4 home). `.E.0` GREEN redefined accordingly (see `.E.0` plan body "Foundation sequence (phased)" subsection).

**Reusable-methodology promotion (per `feedback_audit_canonical_sister_before_new_infra` + `feedback_framework_layer_payoff_diminishing_returns`):** this phased foundation is NOT a new parallel spec — it AMENDS the EXISTING canonical sister `meta-disciplines/audit-driven-sub-sprint-trajectory-verification.md` (D-60; ALREADY `stage: 3-first-canonical` v1.0, established 2026-05-28, landing_ship `.E.0` — the earlier "not-yet-authored" read was a false negative from the missing engine-side symlink, now restored) v1.0→v1.1 by adding the phase structure + the 3 refinements, and it COMPOSES the enforcement-ladder/verifiability-triad (D-70) + rolling-window-seam-cadence (D-72) specs (also promoting at `.E.0` Step-3). Promotion tracked as a `.E.0`-codification deliverable (task #1) so it can't rot. Applies to ANY high-risk multi-ship restructure sub-sprint (criterion per D-60: scope >5 ships OR >4 weeks OR architectural-restructure-exceeds-single-ship).

They are DISPERSED: M1-M7 meta-disciplines (audit-methodology gaps; § 11.5) + B14-B19 pillars (impl-blindspots) + `/capture-audit`+`/metadata-audit` (workspace-drift) + sister-cohort-amendment-completeness (cascade-propagation) + `feedback_*` memories. <!-- D/C/F: D-77 -->
**D-77: Heavier-default audit posture adopted** (operator directive 2026-05-29, empirically grounded). Swap the DEFAULT pre-coding/audit posture to HEAVIER; the LIGHTER pass becomes an EARNED exception, justified only when the touched surface already carries Tier-1/2 deterministic guard coverage (CI gate / `static_assert` / golden-master / determinism+replay gate). **Decision rule:** audit weight ∝ INVERSE deterministic coverage — heavy where the guard-coverage-matrix has a HOLE (the LLM pass is the only guard), light where a deterministic guard is already the floor. **Empirical basis:** the MAJORITY of the existing codebase was audited with the LIGHTER passes + the `.E.0` read-only audit surfaced 141 findings → light passes leak correctness errors at a material rate; engine runs real money (wrong fill loses capital fast) → asymmetry favors heavy; the multiple-passes pain operator felt with light passes IS the variance problem (heavier-but-consistent = LESS total work). Refines `feedback_tiered_audit_discipline_per_plan_scope` (raise default tier; light = earned). Sister: `user_structure_is_correctness_risk_control_for_capital` (burden of proof on REMOVING a control). Codified as memory `feedback_heavier_default_audit_posture_for_capital`. Operationalized in `.E.0.2` + the hardened `/precoding-audit-gate`.
<!-- STATUS: landed (discipline; operationalized in .E.0.2) -->

**D-78: Correctness-apparatus hardening = co-develop/calibrate (not design-in-abstract) + standing ratchet + memory-loop; folded into `.E.0.2`; gated behind guard-matrix completeness** (operator-approved 2026-05-29). The apparatus hardening (hardened `/precoding-audit-gate` as a CONSUMER of the meta-catalog + memory corpus; heavier-default tier per D-77; coverage-gated heavy/light rule) is built by CALIBRATION against real ships, NOT designed in the abstract (abstract hardening = gold-plating against imagined gaps — the failure mode operator's usage-observations warn against). Two grounding sources: **backward** — mine shipped `.D.1`/`.B.8` plans + postmortems + handoffs for "what the gate passed but we caught later"; **forward** — `.E.0.1` as the live calibration bench (the way `/plan-dive` was calibrated on `.E.1`). FOLDED into `.E.0.2` (apparatus-first; the gate-hardening is the catalog's enforcement arm, sibling to cascade-audit Check 12 — do NOT bolt on piecemeal per D-75). **Standing RATCHET:** every ship's close feeds observed gate-gaps back (memory→catalog→gate→harvest loop, permanent); the gate is never "done" but is bounded PER-SURFACE by the guard-coverage-matrix completeness contract — "done enough to code" = matrix-complete (no HOLE) for the touched surface; the floor only rises. **Sequence:** `.E.1`+ implementation stays gated behind apparatus-completeness; `.E.0.1` (foundation; independently protected by its own deterministic gates) co-develops as the calibration bench. → `.E.0.2` plan amended to add this as Piece 4 (the hardened gate) + the apparatus-complete DoD.
<!-- STATUS: landed (approach; .E.0.2 design pass folds in Piece 4 + DoD) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-79 -->
**D-79: Independent deliverable-completeness review added to `/close-session` (operator directive 2026-05-29).** NEW `/close-session` Stage 5.5: at session close, spawn an INDEPENDENT reviewer agent to verify the session's substantive deliverables actually landed COMPLETE + coherent on disk (VERIFIED/PARTIAL/MISSING per item, against the actual files, skeptically). Distinct from Stage 2/4 (mechanical capture-audit drift) + Stage 4.5 (meta-error harvest): content-completeness + cross-artifact-coherence by INDEPENDENT eyes. **Why independence:** the agent that BUILT the work is prone to confirming "I did X" from memory rather than verifying X landed — self-attestation bias; an agent with no stake reports only what's on disk. Close-session analog of the hardened gate's Stage 3.5 verification-pass; sister to `feedback_golden_master_over_reimplemented_oracle` + the meta-anti-pattern-index AR-1 (verify-don't-assume). **Proven value:** surfaced + proven THIS session — the `.E.0.2` build review returned all-VERIFIED + caught a real WH-2 stale-label nit (body still read "v0.1 DRAFT"). **Gating (heavier-default per D-77; light earned):** FIRE for substantial multi-artifact sessions (subsystem/ship/≥~4 artifacts/HIGH-RISK); SKIP trivial (`--no-review`). **Canonical-sister:** EXTENDS `/close-session` (new Stage; NOT a new skill) — forced sister-cohort amendments: stage-list + execution-model carve-out (the ONE spawned sub-agent) + report-template, all updated. Operationalizes the heavier-default posture at the close surface. Itself an instance of "need creates the tool" (the review proved itself this session, so it's codified).
<!-- STATUS: landed (/close-session Stage 5.5 + execution-model carve-out + report-template; D-79) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-80 -->
**D-80: Doc-hygiene cadence structural finding + the session's discipline arc (2026-05-29; `.E.0.2`-close consolidation).** Two interlocking captures from the `.E.0.2` apparatus session, recorded here because they were otherwise only in memories/tasks/catalog/handoff (not the decision narrative):
**(1) Doc-hygiene cadence finding → mechanization layer + task #14.** The doc-system index drifted badly (`DESIGN_SPECS/README.md` listed **6** meta-disciplines; **19** exist on disk) — NOT because the tool is missing (`check_doc_metadata` exists, HAS `check_bidirectional_sisters`, IS wired) but because it runs only at SLOW cadence (`/metadata-audit` quarterly, `/ship` close) and lacks an index-completeness check. **Fix (#14):** extend `check_doc_metadata` with `check_index_completeness` (spec→README / memory→MEMORY.md / skill→CLAUDE.md) + wire into `/close-session` (reliable always-fires cadence) with TWO-TIER resolution — AUTO-`/index-rebuild` the mechanical drift, SURFACE the vocab/taxonomy list for operator judgment (the irreducible manual floor) — + `index_exempt` generated index files. README STILL STALE 6/19 (do NOT hand-patch; #14's first run fixes it). The recursion ("a CI tool for the CI tools?") stops because a detector is terminal: the only question about a checker is "did it run?" = a cadence wiring, not another tool.
**(2) The discipline arc — the agent's pull-toward-closure must be structurally checked.** Four disciplines codified, all facets of ONE lesson (the efficiency-instinct erodes coverage one rationalization at a time; operator pushback held the line every time): **D-77 heavier-default** (light is earned); **`feedback_never_skip_thoroughness_unless_explicit`** (catalog PL-2 — thorough steps fire by DEFAULT; skip is operator-EXPLICIT only; a review fires on UN-reviewed work; instance = skipped Stage 5.5 at close → close-out unverified); **`feedback_independence_for_judgment_not_mechanical`** (mechanical→tool, judgment→independent-agent; mechanizing a judgment-check eliminates its agent); **AR-1 generalized "verify before you conclude"** (concluded "guard dormant"/"done-line" before checking — both wrong). Plus the cross-ref-web bidirectional closure (7 reverse-refs) + `feedback_skill_edit_cohort_checklist`. Mechanization: `/close-session` Stage 5.5 default-fire gating tightened; **task #15** sweeps the rest of the skills' skip-gating to operator-explicit-only.

## Findings / Discoveries

<!-- D/C/F: F-1 -->
**F-1: BinanceAdapter worker thread already exists as canonical sister.** Investigation revealed the existing CoreFrameworks/BinanceAdapter.hpp pattern IS exactly the "API client thread" Architecture A would build. Architecture D/E extends this rather than parallels.
<!-- STATUS: noted (informs `.E.1` plan body) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-2 -->
**F-2: Known existing KS race resolved as side-effect of Architecture E.** Producer thread reads `oms.balance` while drainer writes it. Aggregator-as-single-writer pattern + seqlock-published account state closes this race structurally. Free bonus.
<!-- STATUS: noted (capture in `.E.1` plan body Bug Classes section) -->
<!-- D/C/F: F-2-AMEND -->
**F-2 REFRAMED (2026-06-10, `.E.0.10` adversarial 3-agent sweep) — NOT a "free bonus," it is LOAD-BEARING FOR CAPITAL SAFETY.** The "1 known KS race" is the documented tip of a SYSTEMIC class: **9 sites, the whole OMS money cluster has no seqlock/atomic**, every non-drainer reader reads raw 16B `Money`. **3 are LIVE capital-control paths** — worst is the **reconciler that pushes a balance CORRECTION to live capital off a torn read** (Knight-Capital-shaped), plus the global + per-core MTM kill switches (torn balance/P&L → false-or-missed trips on the capital guard). The aggregator (D-54, single-writer + seqlock-published snapshot) closes the whole CLASS — but because the open sites drive live capital decisions, **`.E.1` is hereby a HARD LIVE-ENABLE GATE: live trading is BLOCKED until the torn-read class + `conc-5` (the distinct SPSC-ring race) land.** Scope discipline: the aggregator closes the torn-read class ONLY; `persist-8` (stale-flag, fixed `d0a6bb1`) and `conc-5` (SPSC ring → single-producer/MPSC fix) are DISTINCT roots — do NOT assume the aggregator closes them. NEW reader-side spec: `concurrency-patterns/cross-thread-multiword-read-consistency-discipline.md`. Full sweep + the over-lump correction: `plans/v5.15-live-readiness/plan_checks/E.0.10-finding-disposition-register.md`.
<!-- STATUS: decided (live-enable gate); folded into `.E.1` plan body Bug Classes + acceptance sections 2026-06-10 -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-3 -->
**F-3: Architecture E's latency analysis — removes drainer middleman; latency IMPROVES vs current.** Current submit path has ~10-100μs p99 variance from drainer cycle. E removes drainer hop entirely. NOT a regression vs current.
<!-- STATUS: noted (`.E.1` plan body Verification gate: latency baseline measurement) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-4 -->
**F-4: Theoretical on-box latency floor ~3-5μs tick-to-wire** with DPDK + kTLS + SHA-NI + isolcpus + hugepages + C-state-disabled. Below = custom hardware territory.
<!-- STATUS: noted (informs `.E.8` plan body scope) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-5 -->
**F-5: Network dominates latency by 3 orders of magnitude.** Architecture changes save microseconds; colocation saves milliseconds. Architecture work = VARIANCE reduction + structural correctness. Colocation = mean reduction.
<!-- STATUS: noted (informs `.E` postmortems framing) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-6 -->
**F-6: Binance WebSocket API for order submission is biggest single-step latency win.** Persistent `/ws-api/v3` connection eliminates TCP+TLS handshake per submit; saves ~15-25ms per submit vs REST-per-request. Lands at `.E.3`.
<!-- STATUS: noted (`.E.3` is highest-ROI ship by single-step latency improvement) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-7 -->
**F-7: Cluster framing is structurally meaningful, not just naming.** Cluster = per-exchange shared resource pool (producer + adapter + WS + sub-accounts). Reflects in filesystem layout. Cluster-level kill switch hierarchy. NEW DESIGN_SPEC pattern.
<!-- STATUS: noted (informs `.E.1` plan body architecture section) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-8 -->
**F-8: Producer is per-cluster, not per-deployment.** Each exchange has its own market-data WS; each cluster needs its own producer thread reading exchange-specific format. Per-cluster isolation = cleaner failure mode (one exchange WS drop doesn't affect others).
<!-- STATUS: noted (informs topology calculations + .E.1 plan body) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-9 -->
**F-9: 24 CPU cores for 2-cluster × 4-node production deployment.** Comfortable on Threadripper/EPYC. Tight on 16-core (1 cluster × 4 nodes = ~14 cores fits). Laptop dev via topology.mode=dev (OS-scheduled threads).
<!-- STATUS: noted (informs operator capacity planning) -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-10 -->
**F-10: Linux_Theme `fox-*` namespace doesn't collide with proposed binary names.** Linux_Theme uses fox-develop / fox-backup / fox-harden / fox-ai-* / fox-test / fox-sandbox. Our names: fox-engine / fox-tui / fox-cli / foxml-train. Clean.
<!-- STATUS: noted -->

<!-- D/C/F: F-11 -->
**F-11: O(1) Event-Sourced Aggregator pattern surfaced via operator research 2026-05-28.** Per Caramel-cited pattern (Concept 1). Push-model aggregator update on each fill instead of pull-model cycle-based scan. Drops kill-switch latency floor by ~2000× (100ms → 50μs). Closes race window between fill arrival and aggregator cycle pickup. Codified as D-54 + NEW DESIGN_SPEC. Sister to existing running-aggregate discipline (RollingStats; oms.balance; per-core core_realized).

Struct-of-Arrays layout + AVX-512 SIMD = 100× speedup for portfolio walks even at small N=8-32. Cache-locality + memory bandwidth wins compound. Bundled with NodeState refactor at `.E.1` (incremental cost). ML feature compute application deferred to `.E.6/.E.7` (substantial wins per-cluster). Codified as D-55. Sister to H10 (AVX-512 SIMD kernel discipline) + existing RollingStats AVX-512 vectorization.

<!-- D/C/F: F-13 -->
**F-13: Asymmetric Blackboard pattern surfaced via operator research 2026-05-28; already adopted.** Per Caramel-cited pattern (Concept 3). Strategy/ML cores write to fixed L3 cache blocks; execution core does blind reads. WE ALREADY DO THIS via seqlock (slow→hot cfg) + atomic flags (kill switch) + per-node→aggregator state publication. Pattern-selection criteria: SPSC for must-not-miss event streams; blackboard for latest-value-wins state visibility. Codified as D-56 (documentation only; no code change). Sister to existing concurrency-patterns/concurrency-model-summary.

## Session 4 (2026-05-30) — money-numeric-core convergence (continuation; `/accept-handoff` of the Session-3 close handoff)

Continuation session. The **D-97 decision was TAKEN (decimal)** and deepened with the operator into a **unified-radix-core architecture** (D-99) + a **golden-epoch invariant** (D-100) + a **sequencing lock** (D-101). No engine code — planning/design + capture. Grounded in 4 read-only agent code-sweeps (hot-path money ops / accounting mul-div / money-type blast radius / FPN API surface). Sisters: `feedback_plan_right_not_fast` · `feedback_motivated_collaborator_for_caramel` · `feedback_single_source_of_truth_discipline` · `feedback_two_foundations_determinism_vs_correctness` · `feedback_structural_fix_for_recurring_class` · `feedback_overengineering_boundary_when_future_easier` · `feedback_close_the_class_vs_migrate_every_site` · `user_structure_is_correctness_risk_control_for_capital`.

<!-- D/C/F: D-99 -->
**D-99: Money-numeric-core = ONE unified radix-parametrized `FixedPoint<RADIX,FRAC>` (operator-driven, 2026-05-30; SUPERSEDES the minimal-`DecimalFPN`-sibling framing of D-97's implementation).** Two parallel money types (binary `FPN_*` + a separate `DecimalFPN_*`) = the **Class-21/SSoT parallel-implementation trap** — Agent-D confirmed **40+ ops** (Add/Sub/Div/compares/Floor/Round/ToString = word-loops) are **radix-AGNOSTIC**, so duplicating them carries no semantic justification (the SSoT "keep-separate" exception is for merges that LOSE information, e.g. H10's SIMD+scalar parity pair — not this). **DECISION:** one `FixedPoint<RADIX,FRAC>` template; **binary (`RADIX=2`** — features/ML/stats) + **decimal (`RADIX=10`** — money) are INSTANTIATIONS sharing the generic bodies; only `Mul` (scale-reduction `>>F` vs `/10^k`) + a few conversions are radix-specific via `if constexpr`. **ABSORBS the parallel `FixedPoint64.hpp` native-128 type** (its own AddSat/Mul/Div + the `USE_NATIVE_128` bridge — itself an SSoT wart + the **F-058 aliasing-pun home**) into `FixedPoint<2,64>` w/ a native-storage policy → kills the parallel type, not just the dup'd decimal ops. **NOT premature abstraction** (two CONCRETE instantiations TODAY, not hypotheticals — operator: "premature abstraction is a plus here, getting it 100% right now is critical"); the {2,10} boundary IS the discipline line — get the core 100% right, do NOT over-parametrize with ROUNDING/OVERFLOW policy params no instantiation uses (THAT would be the §1.5 anti-pattern). **NOT a from-scratch full rewrite either** — the resolution of operator's "isn't a rewrite less risky given our CI/catalog?": post-`.E.0.1` there are **NO known bugs left in the binary bodies** (F-056/57/58/78 ARE those bugs; `.E.0.1` fixes+locks them), so a body rewrite would re-do FP-determinism debugging `.E.0.1` already did. **= "aggressive structural rewrite that REUSES determinism-certified bodies"**: rewrite the FRAME + absorb FP64 + write the DECIMAL fresh against the anti-pattern catalog/DESIGN_SPECS; reuse the certified binary bodies. Captures 100% of the rewrite's upside (one coherent core, all known classes designed out) because the only bug-bearing code is what's written fresh anyway. Domain split (D-97): money→decimal; features/ML/stats→binary (need Exp/Sqrt/Log + magnitude-independent precision, NOT decimal-exactness).
<!-- STATUS: decided (direction); deep design = focused numeric-foundation ship (HIGH-RISK; its own /precoding-audit-gate + /blindspot-scan + new-fn design-audit per D-93) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-100 -->
**D-100: Golden-EPOCH invariant + determinism≠correctness one-time boundary gate (operator-derived, 2026-05-30; REFINES D-82).** The determinism invariant is **scoped to a core EPOCH**: within an epoch, byte-reproducibility (cross-run/binary/locale) is ABSOLUTE; the epoch transition (binary→decimal core) is a **DELIBERATE, RECORDED event** (bump version + regen the characterization golden + retrain models). **before≠after is NOT a regression — it IS the epoch boundary**; after≠match-going-forward WOULD be the regression. That scoping is the invariant (operator: "anything going forward after a core change must match — that's the ideal of an invariant"). **CRITICAL ADD: determinism ≠ correctness** — a WRONG-but-deterministic decimal impl reproduces itself byte-for-byte forever (the determinism gate blesses a consistent wrong answer). So the regen boundary needs a **ONE-TIME CORRECTNESS GATE** determinism cannot provide: decimal-exactness differential (`0.1+0.2==0.3` exactly) + fees-to-the-penny vs a hand-computed reference + round-trip `parse→emit→parse`. Validate ONCE at regen → freeze → determinism guards it thereafter. **NUANCE vs `golden_master_over_reimplemented_oracle`:** a one-time differential vs an EXTERNAL decimal reference (Python `decimal`/bignum) at the boundary is LEGIT (one-shot validation, frozen to golden) — distinct from a STANDING reimplemented oracle that drifts+lies. Permitted-now-because: nothing live depends on the old values yet (SAME precondition as the HMAC/old-model retrain accept — both flip once running capital on prior models).
<!-- STATUS: decided; refines D-82; codify → extend memory feedback_two_foundations_determinism_vs_correctness (DONE this session) + DESIGN_SPEC at the task-#4 codification batch -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-101 -->
**D-101: Sequencing — the numeric core reaches FINAL shape BEFORE the `.E.1` ~5,000-site rename (operator "more work now = less later", 2026-05-30; = overengineering-boundary + D-73 + D-82).** Order: **(1)** D-97/D-99 decision [≈done via this session] → **(2)** `.E.0.1` certifies the CURRENT binary core deterministic (F-056/57/58 + locale-class + H12; the D-97-UNAFFECTED parts — features stay binary) → locks the golden = **THE NET** → **(3)** NEW numeric-foundation unification ship: radix-unify `FixedPoint<RADIX,FRAC>` + absorb `FixedPoint64` + add decimal money + migrate (4 parse / ~30 money cfg / 42 accounting sites per the sweeps) + stamp retrain, **UNDER the net** (binary instantiation byte-identical to step-2 golden = red-build gate; money paths regen goldens deliberately w/ the D-100 correctness gate) → **(4)** `.E.1` rename on the FINAL numeric shape (core touched ONCE). The net (step 2) makes the step-3 rewrite SAFE (D-73 lock-current-first; a full-body rewrite that changed representation + fixed determinism + restructured all at once would have no stable golden to diff = the "can't tell what broke" failure D-82 prevents). **OPEN (for the doc):** (a) the numeric-foundation ship's slot/name — own `.E.0.x` vs the reshaped `.E.0.3` (lean: it IS / subsumes `.E.0.3`, which was already the deterministic-IO/parse-foundation ship — but the TYPE-unification is bigger than `.E.0.3`'s parse scope, so possibly `.E.0.3` folds INTO it); (b) decimal parse-family fold (same parse surface → lean fold). **SUPERSEDED by decimal+unified-core:** D-85 (binary-direct) FULLY; D-96 (POW10_RECIP scaling apparatus — MOOT: decimal parse = digit-accumulate-into-scaled-int, no reciprocal table); D-94 ingestion-parse target is now DECIMAL (still fixed-cost per H20, and naturally simpler).
<!-- STATUS: decided (sequence); OPEN: numeric-foundation ship slot/name + parse-family fold → resolve in the foundational design doc -->

### Audit ratification (3 agents fired 2026-05-30: /accounting-audit + price/qty chain-trace + ingest-architecture) — 4 design-shaping findings → D-102..D-105

<!-- D/C/F: D-102 -->
**D-102 (FINDING, audit-surfaced): the producer double round-trip is a LATENT precision-loss seam that the decimal move MUST close — and it's lossy for BINARY today too.** The ingest-architecture agent found the parsed value does NOT travel straight from `FPN_FromString` into the per-node `Tick` ring. Producer parses `string→FPN` at `BinanceCrypto.hpp:744-745`, but then passes the **cached doubles** (`ds.price_d`/`volume_d`) to fan_out (`Run.hpp:1374`), and `EngineSharded_Async_FanOut` **re-derives FPN via `FPN_FromDouble`** at `Async.hpp:179-180`. Net path to every node = `FPN_FromDouble(FPN_ToDouble(FPN_FromString(string)))` — a **string→FPN→double→FPN round-trip that discards the exact parse through a 52-bit double**, on every tick to every node. So "convert once at source" is actually TWO actions: (a) parse-to-decimal at `:744-745`, AND (b) **carry the parsed value straight into the `Tick` ring** (`Tick.hpp:31-43`) instead of re-deriving from `price_d` — kill the `Run.hpp:1374`→`Async.hpp:179-180` double detour. This is a pre-existing precision bug (binary loses the exact parse here NOW); the decimal move is the forcing function to close it. → folds into the numeric-foundation ship's ingest phase; also a TECH_DEBT row (latent-today).
<!-- STATUS: finding captured; fix = carry-decimal-through-ring in the numeric-foundation ship -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-103 -->
**D-103 (DESIGN, audit-surfaced): the decimal↔binary boundary is a PAIR of type-INVISIBLE casts, enumerated by hand — not one cast.** The chain-trace found there is **no type-level fork today** — money and features are BOTH `FPN<F>`; the domain split is semantic over one shared type. And the boundary runs **backwards** from the naive expectation: feature outputs (`rolling->price_avg`/`price_stddev`/`price_max`/`ema_price`) **BECOME** the money gate thresholds. So two cast surfaces: **(1) decimal→binary at feature INGRESS** — the one chokepoint `EventLoop_UpdateRollingStateOneCore` → `RollingStats_Push` (`ControllerEventLoop.hpp:2194-2197`; + the EMA blend `PortfolioController.hpp:943-945`; + legacy/backtest dupes); **(2) binary→decimal at threshold EGRESS** — `StrategyParameters.hpp:347/428/511/634` (`out->bg_price_threshold`/`sg_take_profit_price`/`sg_stop_loss_price` = feature-derived) + the per-tick mixed sites in `PortfolioController.hpp` (danger band `:1048-1049`/`:1585-1586`, EMA blend `:943-945`, gate reprice `:1037-1041`, no-trade band `:859-873`). **CRITICAL HAZARD: all these are FPN-vs-FPN TODAY → they compile SILENTLY after the type change → they will NOT surface as type errors.** They must be EXHAUSTIVELY ENUMERATED from the chain-trace's §3 list before coding (per `feedback_enumerate_set_before_categorical_claim` — the type system won't catch a missed one). The hot path itself (sharded `ExecutionCore_Tick_Impl`) does ONLY money compares → price stays pure-decimal there; the EMA/feature mixing lives on slow/producer paths (the legacy centralized `PortfolioController_Tick` co-locates them — its own per-tick cast if retained). Design implication: making the boundary a STRONGLY-TYPED cast (distinct decimal vs binary types, explicit `to_binary()`/`to_decimal()`) converts the silent-compile hazard into a compile-ERROR at each boundary = the structural close (turns D-103's hazard into H-invariant-grade enforcement).
<!-- STATUS: design captured; exhaustive boundary enumeration = a numeric-foundation-ship pre-coding deliverable; strong-typing the boundary is the structural close -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-104 -->
**D-104 (DESIGN, audit-ratified): single global decimal scale `10^8`; per-symbol metadata is order-side-only; + decimal naturally fills the MISSING tick/lot quantization (a live-trading prerequisite).** Ingest agent confirmed Binance sends ≤8 decimals for price AND qty across the USD-quote universe (codebase helpers assume ≤8-10dp; BTC qty step `1e-6`, price `1e-2`) → **one fixed `10^8` scale represents every symbol EXACTLY** — per-symbol storage scales buy nothing for exactness (resolves operator Q1: no per-symbol scale, and with a compile-time `10^8` not even warmup-inference is needed; per-symbol min-tick assertion only becomes relevant if multi-symbol later). Per-symbol precision metadata DOES exist but qty-only + order-side-only (`SymbolFilters` `BinanceOrderAPI.hpp:75-82`; `lot_step_size`/`qty_decimals` from exchangeInfo; = TECH_DEBT-146) — needed for **order rounding** regardless of storage scale. **GAP the audit surfaced: there is NO `tickSize`/`price_decimals`/PRICE_FILTER anywhere + NO price/qty quantization in the engine at all** (MARKET-only today, so no limit-price rounding needed — but a live-trading prerequisite the moment limit orders or stricter venues land). Decimal makes quantization NATURAL (`FixedPoint<10,8>` → round-to-N-decimals = the exchange's precision) → add `FPN_Quantize(value, decimals)` at order submit; closes TECH_DEBT-146 by making `binance_round_qty` an exact decimal-truncate-to-step.
<!-- STATUS: decided (10^8 global scale); FPN_Quantize at submit folds into the numeric-foundation ship; closes TECH_DEBT-146 -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-105 -->
**D-105 (DESIGN, audit-surfaced): ONE canonical rounding mode applied UNIFORMLY at every money site INCLUDING replay — the single hard determinism requirement; mode-choice is a research item.** Accounting agent confirmed: there is **NO rounding mode anywhere today** — `FPN_Mul` (`FixedPointN.hpp:583`) truncates-toward-zero at bit F; `FPN_DivNoAssert` (`:687`) truncates the quotient; **all ~42 money mul/div sites silently truncate**. The engine is internally self-consistent (entry/exit/replay all truncate the same way → balances reconcile, replay byte-identical against itself) — so this is a **correctness-WIN, not a present bug** (0 CRITICAL / 4 HIGH). The HARD requirement for the decimal move: pick ONE canonical decimal rounding mode and apply it at EVERY money site **including `Portfolio_FromEventLog` replay** (`ControllerEventLoop.hpp:862-890`) — else live≠replay and recovery-parity breaks (the D-100 epoch-golden must be regenerated once, uniformly). **Mode CHOICE (half-even vs match-Binance's-fee-rounding vs truncate) = a RESEARCH item for the doc** (match the exchange's fee convention so our books agree to the penny; operator Q6). The decimal-sensitive cohort (must move together): the realized-PnL+balance chain — `handle_sell_fill` (`OrderManager.hpp:1186-1194`) + production twin `EventLoop_OnEvent` exit (`ControllerEventLoop.hpp:1959-1967`) + `Portfolio_CloseSlot` gross (`Portfolio.hpp:389-392`) + the two fee muls + the kill-switch-boundary `ExitBuffer_PendingProceeds` (`Portfolio.hpp:200-202`) + replay. Hot-path claim RE-CONFIRMED: the 3 hot money-muls (`ExecutionCore.hpp:543/549/570`) are rare-entry-branch only; the 500ns steady path does money COMPARES, not mul/div → decimal does NOT touch hot-path cost.
<!-- STATUS: decided (uniform-rounding requirement incl. replay); rounding-MODE choice = doc research item -->

### D-98 backlog slice for this cohort (the money/FP/parse items → their structural `.E` home)

Per D-98 (sweep the open backlog at each ship's planning), the FP/accounting/parse/money slice of the open ledger maps as:

### The generalization — "let the venue decide" → D-106

<!-- D/C/F: D-106 -->
**D-106 (DESIGN, operator-driven 2026-05-30): the VENUE is SSoT for numeric semantics — "let the venue decide"; per-venue semantics → FOREACH_EXCHANGE registry; storage = fixed compile-time scale ≥ max-venue-precision + a guard. Generalizes D-104/D-105; composes D-3.** Operator: "match binance or whatever exchange/venue we're trading on — we never have to make a decision, we just let the venue decide." This is D-97 source-fidelity GENERALIZED into a principle: every internal numeric convention that DUPLICATES an external authority's choice is (1) a decision we didn't have to make + (2) a silent drift source (our convention vs theirs). Defer → removes both. **Resolution of storage-vs-semantics:**
> **(a) STORAGE scale = a fixed COMPILE-TIME constant ≥ every supported venue's precision.** Binance ≤8dp → `10^8` is the Binance instantiation of "match the widest venue" (a wider scale is still EXACT for a narrower venue = trailing zeros). NOT runtime-per-venue — keep the type clean (D-99's no-over-parametrize boundary; a runtime exponent = a slower scaled-decimal, the §1.5 anti-pattern).
> **(b) per-venue SEMANTICS = registry metadata** — `price_decimals` / `qty_decimals` / `tickSize` / `stepSize` / **fee-rounding-mode** as a **FOREACH_EXCHANGE row per venue** (composes with D-3 multi-exchange-from-`.E.1`) → adding a venue = 1 row (the framework philosophy applied to numeric semantics).
> **(c) GUARD: `static_assert`/boot-check `storage_decimals ≥ venue_precision`** per exchange row → a venue needing MORE precision than storage FAILS LOUD, never silently truncates money. Same shape as the phantom-invariant close (D-92): READ + GUARD the invariant from the source, never ASSUME it.

**REFINES D-105's rounding open-item:** the mode is no longer a philosophical pick (half-even vs truncate) — it's "match the VENUE's fee-rounding convention, per-venue, in the registry." The research item becomes concrete: **"determine Binance's actual fee-rounding convention"** (a fact to READ, not a choice to make). **REFINES D-104:** `10^8` stays correct as the Binance value; the framing is now "fixed ≥ max-venue + per-venue semantics-in-registry + guard," multi-venue-ready. Codified: memory `feedback_defer_to_source_authority_for_external_semantics` (NEW this session).
<!-- STATUS: decided; storage=fixed-compile-time-scale + venue-semantics-in-FOREACH_EXCHANGE + storage≥precision guard; rounding = venue-matched (research Binance convention); composes D-3 -->

### Open-decision settlement (O-1/O-2/R-1) + completeness sweep → D-107..D-110 (operator: "settle these now, make sure we haven't forgotten anything")

<!-- D/C/F: D-107 -->
**D-107 (O-1 SETTLED — YES, strong-type the money/binary boundary).** Make money (`FixedPoint<10,8>`) and binary (`FixedPoint<2,64>`) genuinely DISTINCT types with NO implicit cross-radix conversion — only explicit, named, DETERMINISTIC `to_binary()` / `to_decimal()` casts. **Key realization:** the two are ALREADY distinct template instantiations, so "strong-typing" = simply *don't provide* an implicit cross-radix operator → mixing them in one expression is a compile-ERROR **by default**; the work is providing the explicit named conversions + applying them at the ~12 D-103 boundary sites. **This converts D-103's silent-compile hazard into compiler-enumerated enforcement** — the compiler finds every boundary site for us (no need to hand-enumerate + hope; `feedback_enumerate_set_before_categorical_claim` satisfied structurally). Aligns H13 (typed over punning) + compile-time-enforcement gradient + structural-fix-preferred. Cost = ~12 explicit casts; that friction IS the correctness (can't accidentally mix money + feature math). Constraints: the casts are SINGLE-SOURCE named fns (not scattered inline) so they're auditable; the decimal→binary feature cast is byte-DETERMINISTIC (train-serve M5 + the one-time retrain per D-97). Parametric/generic code templated over the numeric type is UNAFFECTED — only mixed-domain expressions error, which are exactly the boundary. = the structural close of the "two-domains-one-type silent-cast" candidate class (the doc's anti-pattern #1).
<!-- STATUS: settled YES; implement as distinct types + explicit deterministic casts in the numeric-foundation ship -->

So for LIVE, "let the venue decide" is literal: **book the reported commission (source-exact)**; fee-rounding-MODE only matters for PAPER/BACKTEST fidelity (compute with round-UP-at-asset-precision to match what Binance would charge). **Venue row (`FOREACH_EXCHANGE` Binance):** `fee_rounding = ROUND_UP`, `fee_precision = per-symbol base/quoteAssetPrecision` (EXTENDS the existing `SymbolFilters`/exchangeInfo load — D-104 already pulls exchangeInfo for lot/notional; add the precision fields). **Empirical binding check** = computed-vs-reported fee on RECORDED LIVE fills (the engine records fills → the data exists; this is the golden-master confirmation, sister to D-100). **NEW FINDINGS:**

[grep inconclusive this session — the string `commission` could not be confirmed present; tool rendering was flaky. The very-likely state, given the audit, is that the engine COMPUTES rather than books-reported.] → VERIFY at the ship; if computed, switch LIVE to book the reported commission. > **F-B (note, pre-existing): fee-ASSET denomination.** Binance charges the BUY fee in the BASE asset (e.g. BTC), but the engine computes a quote-denominated fee (`notional * fee_rate`). If so, the quote-denomination is a modeling APPROXIMATION (pre-existing; not decimal-introduced). Low priority; note for the accounting re-check. **D-122 (C1 + B-ε RESOLVED: hoist the int128 double-width mul into the unified template as the SHARED primitive — binary `<2,64>` byte-identical by construction; + the golden-lifecycle refinement of D-100).** **C1 → HOIST (Option A).** The int128 `__uint128_t` double-width mul (today's certified FP64 body) becomes the SHARED mul primitive for BOTH radices — radix-specific only in the reduce step (`>>FRAC` binary vs `/10^FRAC` decimal); byte-identical to the `.E.0.1` golden for binary BY CONSTRUCTION (it IS the certified arithmetic) → zero feature-model retrain, zero binary-golden regen. `FPN_Sqrt<64>` stays generic (F-056). **Rejected B** (generic-for-binary): relies on the generic≡native empirical bet (verified 19 ops, not proven), regresses latency (word-array vs native uint128 on feature math), splits into two mul machineries — and per the operator's end-goal framing its bigger blast (a feature epoch) does NOT advance the end goal (features need determinism + magnitude-independent precision, which binary already gives; only MONEY needs decimal). **Boldness = end-goal-advancement, not amount-changed.** **Rejected C** (full rewrite) per D-99. **Effort-check (operator-requested):** A is the substantial-work option, NOT effort-minimizing — the lazy path was B; the harder alternatives (full rewrite / "generic byte-identical-and-fast") are harder-AND-worse (re-certify for nothing / collapse into a latency regression or into A). **B-ε resolved (same decision):** native-128 = the COMPUTE policy; stored/wire/snapshot stays the canonical 2-word 24B layout (else binary byte-identity breaks). **Golden-lifecycle refinement of D-100 (two-foundations D-82 applied to the golden):** binary uses ONE FIXED golden (the `.E.0.1` FP-golden) continuously through #11, NO regen (structural byte-preserving refactor); money has NO pre-existing golden — a characterization baseline guards run-to-run REPRODUCIBILITY continuously (re-frozen at each DELIBERATE money-behavior change), and the CORRECTNESS-validated golden is frozen ONCE at lock-in vs the external oracle (Python `decimal`). determinism ≠ correctness: the between-updates golden catches accidental non-determinism; the at-lock oracle catches wrong-but-consistent values; both needed, in order. The EPOCH is confined to the decimal money type; the binary FPN headers are a STRUCTURAL refactor.

<!-- D/C/F: D-123 -->
**D-123 (C4 + C3 RESOLVED: decimal `OrderResult`, source-exact `string→decimal` parse at the adapter; book the exchange-reported commission source-exact).** **C4 → decimal `OrderResult`.** The Binance fill fields arrive as JSON STRINGS (`"L"`/`"l"` price/qty, `BinanceUserData.hpp:304-305`) but are currently `binance_json_extract_double`'d (`:338`) → lossy at `string→double` BEFORE any downstream cast, then re-derived into realized PnL = the AP2 lossy-carry on the capital fill path. Fix: parse `"L"`/`"l"` DIRECTLY to decimal `<10,8>` (digit-accumulate — exact AND simpler than the double path) + carry decimal on `OrderResult`. The "keep double + guarded cast" alternative is lossy-by-construction (double can't hold 8-dp venue values exactly → the guard protects an already-wrong value); source-exact reconciliation requires `string→decimal` where the string still exists. No latency cost (WS-thread parse + drainer cadence, NOT the 500ns path). **C3 folds in (same decision):** the commission `"n"` is also a STRING → parse `string→decimal` → BOOK that (source-exact, D-106 venue-as-SSoT) = C3's "LIVE books the reported commission, not a `notional*fee_rate` recompute" fix (F-A confirmed `OrderManager.hpp:1142-1144`). So `OrderResult` carries exchange-reported price/qty/commission as exact decimal, parsed source-exact. **`.E.1` cross-plan impact (captured here in the shared `.E` decision log — which `.E.1` reads at pickup):** the decimal-`OrderResult` shape IS the ExchangeAdapter contract; every multi-exchange adapter `.E.1` builds inherits it (→ `exchange-adapter-implementation-contract.md` updates at #11 close). **Spec touch (draft step-3 / lock at #11 close per pattern-codification-lifecycle):** AP2 fill-path instance (extends Class 27) + adapter-contract spec + `domain-split-representation`.
<!-- STATUS: decided (C4=decimal OrderResult source-exact + C3 book-reported-commission); .E.1 adapter contract noted; feeds #11 design -->

Computed alongside binary rolling stats (`rolling->price_avg`) via slopes/spreads/ratios/comparisons = feature-domain math needing determinism + magnitude-independent precision, NOT decimal-exactness → `ema_price` = binary `<2,64>`. The money→binary cast is at the PRICE-INGRESS to the rolling/feature domain (the D-103 boundary already in the blast radius — `RollingStats_Push` `ControllerEventLoop.hpp:2194-2197`), NOT a new one; the producer computes `ema_price` in binary post-cast. Resolves hft-F2 (binary EMA → NO decimal `u128/10^8` libcall on the ≤200ns producer fan_out) AND is the correct domain. One-directional boundary: money-price → binary-ema; `ema_price` never flows back to money (no reverse cast). **All 4 #11 structural forks now RESOLVED: C1=A hoist (D-122) · C4+C3 source-exact decimal OrderResult (D-123) · H2 binary-ema (this). → step-4 COMPLETE; next = step-5 design pass.**

### Session 6 — #11 step-5 design pass: numeric-core representation settled (16B two's-complement) + verified (2026-05-31)

Step-5 design pass (post-`/accept-handoff`). Compounding design sequence: #1 the type → #2 shared multiply → #3 `divmul_pow10` → #4 rounding helper → #5 parse/casts → #6 quantize → #7 fees → #8 D-100 oracle. **This session nailed #1 (the type) + a deep numeric-width exploration; #2-#8 remain.**

<!-- D/C/F: D-125 -->
**D-125 (NEW: adopt 16B TWO'S-COMPLEMENT storage for the unified `FixedPoint<RADIX,FRAC>`, value-equivalence-netted, sequenced BEFORE the money migration).** Switch canonical storage from sign-magnitude (24B: `w[2]` 128-bit magnitude + separate `int32 sign` + `int32 _padding`; packs 2/cache-line WITH straddle since 24∤64) to two's-complement `__int128` 64.64 (16B: sign in top bit; exactly 4/cache-line, no straddle) → ~2× cache-line density on the array-dense binary FEATURE paths, zero split-line loads. Sequencing (D-73): binary-compaction FIRST → money-migration SECOND, so each foundational change keeps a stable diff-anchor (never both unanchored = the "can't tell what broke" failure D-82 prevents). **Net = VALUE-equivalence (NOT byte-equivalence — bytes change by design): decode the `.E.0.1` golden's values through old (sign-mag) and new (two's-comp) engines, assert numbers identical; + fresh byte-determinism re-cert on the 16B format (cheap — `.E.0.1` gate infra exists; regen-and-run, not build-the-net).** Value-PRESERVING → models RE-STAMP not retrain. Rejected: (a) coupling the re-representation INTO #11 (loses the diff-anchor — two foundational rewrites at once on capital code; balloons blast radius to every op body + byte-layout surface); (b) deferring to a far-future ship (it's CORE — touch-once per D-101). REVERSIBLE (keeping sign-mag now doesn't foreclose it).
<!-- STATUS: decided; 16B two's-complement, value-equiv-netted, binary-compaction precedes #11 money; reversible -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-126 -->
**D-126 (16B Step-0 VERIFIED — transcendentals clean, no sign-semantics breakage, range robust for BTC; feature-saturation = full-confidence resolves the high-price tail).** Pre-commit verification (subagent + operator insight): **(1) Transcendentals CLEAN PORT** — `Sqrt`/`Exp`/`Sin`/`Cos` already isolate sign via `abs`/`Negate`/sign-check, NOT magnitude-punning → abs-first two's-complement reproduces them identically (`FPN_Sqrt<64>` stays generic NR per F-056). **(2) NO symmetric-range / neg-zero dependency** — code already collapses ±0; single-zero is a CLOSER match. **(3) Range** — two's-complement 64.64 has 63 integer bits (ONE LESS than sign-mag's 64). Robust for BTC (worst intermediate — variance `ss_total` `n·Σy²`, `RollingStats.hpp:377-378` — ~52 bits @ $70k, ~11 bits headroom). High-price thin spot (~60 bits @ $1M, 3-bit margin, NO clamp) **RESOLVED by the domain split + feature-saturation semantics (operator):** after #11 the binary type holds ONLY features (money=decimal, venue-range-guarded), and for a FEATURE, ceiling-saturation = "max signal / full confidence in the extreme" is CORRECT, not a bug — 62-63 bits is an absurd decision gradient. Direct-magnitude features (variance) saturate fail-SAFE (huge→volatile→defensive); RATIO features (R²/slope) get graceful saturation (detect ceiling → emit full-confidence-extreme, not garbage ratio). **4 small action items (named, not "minor"):** (1) graceful ratio-feature saturation; (2) `−2^63` `Negate`/`Abs` guard (per `FPN_FromInt:254`); (3) port the `USE_NATIVE_128` `FP64` backend (`FixedPoint64.hpp`) in lockstep — the port is TWO files; (4) one-line audit `LargeTradeState_Push` gets base-qty not notional (`FlowFeatures.hpp:346,352`).
<!-- STATUS: decided/verified; PROCEED with 16B; 4 small action items tracked for the compaction ship -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-127 -->
**D-127 (fees = TWO distinct numbers; F-B fee-asset handling FOLDED IN, not deferred — refines D-109).** (a) **BOOKED fee** (accounting/ledger) = exact: LIVE books the exchange-REPORTED commission (source-exact, D-106/D-109), PAPER computes (round-up-at-precision). (b) **DECISION fee** (SL/TP/breakeven LEVELS) = deliberately CONSERVATIVE (operator): use the taker rate (worst case) as a uniform buffer across backtest/paper/live → never under-provision for costs at a decision threshold (small edge cost, orthogonal to accounting exactness). Two numbers, two jobs. **F-B (commission reported in base/quote/BNB asset) FOLDED INTO the design (operator rejected "minor"-defer = PL-4):** book asset-aware — quote = direct, base (BUY) = via fill price (exact), BNB = needs BNB/quote price → NAME the dependency / fail-loud, never silently approximate.
<!-- STATUS: decided; refines D-109; F-B folded in (no defer) -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-128 -->
**D-128 (rounding mode — closes D-105's open "MODE = research item" subpart).** Venue-matched WHERE the venue defines it (fees → round-up-at-precision per D-109; tick/lot → quantize) — there venue-match IS most-accurate (reproduces reality). Internal reduces the venue doesn't govern (notional, PnL) use round-HALF-EVEN (unbiased; in exact-integer there's no cancellation, so the choice is purely the tie-rule). Same mode EVERYWHERE INCLUDING replay (D-105 uniform-rounding requirement). Operator "whichever is most accurate; venue-match ideal" → resolve to the same rule (venue-match externally = most accurate; half-even internally = most accurate where there's no venue to match).
<!-- STATUS: decided; closes D-105 open rounding-MODE subpart -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-129 -->
**D-129 (further numeric-width compression = FUTURE; keep the 64.64 buffer NOW).** Beyond the 16B compaction (D-125), three width ideas PARKED with rationale (operator: "leave this massive buffer for predictions for now"): **(a) 8B (int64, 8/line)** — DEAD at current math (author's `LinearRegression3X.hpp:12-14` overflow history; `n·Σy²` is 52-60 bits); reachable ONLY via a stats-core restructure (mean-centering/Welford OR the operator's per-site-scale / static block-float — both converge on storing deviations-not-prices for the variance driver); PURE density (exact-integer = no cancellation → no accuracy upside), NON-value-preserving (Welford's running-mean division rounds → exact→approximate → new goldens + re-cert), trades away exact-integer-no-resync (`RollingStats.hpp:74-76`); pursue ONLY if density binds. **(b) per-instrument width** (8B for small-magnitude assets — a $5 stock's `n·Σy²` ~15 bits; 4B too tight — precision floor ~20 frac bits + integer bits > 32) = MULTI-ASSET FUTURE (`.E.1`+, composes D-3; template already supports per-`FRAC`/width). **(c) sig-fig-aware bit compression** (store only significant bits, drop dead precision) = further future. **For NOW: keep the 64.64 / 16B headroom as prediction/ML buffer; compress deliberately later, not preemptively.** Tracked in task #12.
<!-- STATUS: decided (future-deferred WITH rationale + keep-buffer-now); 8B/per-instrument/sig-fig parked -->
